provenance-commit	false
provenance-object-format	sha1
provenance-digest-expressible	false
provenance-commit-why	.git/HEAD is "ref: refs/heads/main", which names a branch and no commit; rev-list over it resolves to nothing, so there is no commit to read; and the object format is "sha1", which voxel_model::Digest refuses to express, so a commit here would not be readable by this project even once one exists
provenance-gix		deferred: read-only gix provenance is adopted when a commit exists, and this repository is not a commit yet. The working-tree digest is what stands today (R-59.1)

placement-is-not	a claim that a placed line is understood. It is a claim about where a line's file is named: a file no record names is a file whose history is in no record, and a file named by a record that only read it is placed by a reader, not by a change. The record ids are printed with the counts so that difference stays visible.
